# Visual Plan — 25 One-Paycheck Jobs That Could Buy a House in 1960s America

Generated from the locked script and the Visual Style Profile (STATE 7). Every beat is 3–5 s max and every image prompt is fully standalone.

## Part A — Mix check

```
Beats: 576 (~35 min 46 s) | 🎞️ film 57 | 📷 photo 96 | 🎬 stock 49 | 🔢 graphic 137 | 🎨 AI 237 (B&W 112, Kodachrome 97, recreation 28)
Real share: 35% ✅ (target ≥35%, mix A) | AI share: 41% ✅ (target ≤50%) | Graphics: 24%
Max AI beats in a row: 3 ✅
```

**Finish on every shot:** film grain + dust/scratches, light vignette, occasional orange light leak, slow Ken Burns push on stills, big semi-transparent entry number (#25…#1) on screen for the whole entry.

**Upload reminder:** this video contains realistic AI images → tick YouTube's **"altered or synthetic content" = Yes**.

**Use:** a switchboard operator; slow push-in.  
**Fallback AI prompt:** 1950s black and white documentary photograph, a switchboard operator, slow push-in, medium shot at eye level, flat overhead fluorescent light, warm nostalgic mood, high contrast with deep blacks and bright silvers, heavy film grain, slight softness, 35mm Kodak Tri-X, candid, no text, no logos, no readable signs  
**Camera:** medium shot at eye level · **Lighting:** flat overhead fluorescent light · **Mood:** warm nostalgic · **Action:** slow push-in

#### Beat 569 (~3s) — 📷 ARCHIVAL PHOTO
**Script:** "I want to know if the autoworkers"  
**Get it:** Library of Congress · FSA/OWI (1935–44) → search `automobile worker`  
**Fallback:** Library of Congress (loc.gov/free-to-use) → `automobile assembly line` · or the AI prompt below  
**Use:** an autoworker on the line; slow push-in.  
**Fallback AI prompt:** 1950s black and white documentary photograph, an autoworker on the line, slow push-in, medium shot at eye level, flat overhead fluorescent light, proud and dignified mood, high contrast with deep blacks and bright silvers, heavy film grain, slight softness, 35mm Kodak Tri-X, candid, no text, no logos, no readable signs  
**Camera:** medium shot at eye level · **Lighting:** flat overhead fluorescent light · **Mood:** proud and dignified · **Action:** slow push-in

#### Beat 570 (~3s) — 📷 ARCHIVAL PHOTO
**Script:** "get more love than the coal miners,"

**Fallback:** National Archives (catalog.archives.gov) → `DOCUMERICA coal miner` · or the AI prompt below

**Fallback AI prompt:** 1950s black and white documentary photograph, a coal miner, slow push-in, medium shot at eye level, flat overcast light, proud and dignified mood, high contrast with deep blacks and bright silvers, heavy film grain, slight softness, 35mm Kodak Tri-X, candid, no text, no logos, no readable signs

**Script:** "and whether anyone out there still remembers the sound"

**Camera:** close-up detail shot · **Lighting:** cool blue dawn light with a warm glow on the horizon · **Mood:** warm nostalgic · **Action:** the light warms

#### Beat 574 (~3s) — 🎨 AI IMAGE · 1960s Kodachrome

#### Beat 575 (~5s) — 📷 ARCHIVAL PHOTO

### AI to generate: 237 images
- 112 black & white era photos, 97 Kodachrome colour, 28 cinematic recreations (prompts in Part B). Motion prompts come in STATE 9.
- 137 graphic boards to build in CapCut.
